2020 年報
ANNUAL REPORT
                                                      Corporate Introduction
                                                            公司簡介

Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited (“Kingdee International” or “Kingdee”) was established in 1993. It is listed on the Main Board of the
Hong Kong Stock Exchange (stock code: 0268.HK) and headquartered in Shenzhen, the PRC. Adhering to the core values of “Acting in all Conscience, with
Integrity and Righteousness”, the Company is committed to helping businesses achieve their growth targets and let the sun shine on every company through
dedicated services. It strives to provide them with the most trusted enterprise service platform.

Through persistent efforts to explore China’s Cloud enterprise service market, Kingdee has retained the largest share in the enterprise application software
sector for fast-growing enterprises for 16 consecutive years, and has grasped the biggest share in the enterprise-grade SaaS Cloud services industry for the 4
years. Kingdee is currently the only SaaS cloud service provider of Chinese enterprises selected into Gartner’s global market guide, and has become the only
Chinese SaaS company winning the 2020 IDC SaaS Customer Satisfaction Award.

In addition, Kingdee’s diverse Cloud services and products are the preferred choices of leading enterprises. They include “Kingdee Cloud Cosmic”
(digitalization and ecosystem platform for large enterprises), “Kingdee Cloud Galaxy” (intelligent growth service platform for medium-sized enterprises),
“Kingdee Cloud Stellar” (intelligent growth service platform for micro and small-sized enterprises), “Cloud-Hub” (intelligent Cloud office), “Guanyi Cloud”
(Cloud services for E-commerce operators), “Cargeer” (Cloud services for auto dealers) and “Wojia Cloud” (Cloud services for Property Industry). With its
strengths in management software and Cloud services, Kingdee provides services and products to more than 6.8 million enterprises, government agencies
and other organizations around the world.

金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司（「金蝶國際」或「金蝶」
                       ）始創於1993年，是香港聯交所主板上市公司（股票代碼：0268.HK），總部位於
中國深圳。以「致良知、走正道、行王道」為核心價值觀，以「全心全意為企業服務，讓陽光照進每一個企業」為使命，致力成
為「最值得託付的企業服務平台」。

金蝶在中國企業雲服務市場不斷探索，IDC資料顯示，金蝶不僅連續16年穩居中國成長型企業應用軟件市場佔有率第一，更連
續4年在中國企業級ERM SaaS（即雲ERP）、財務SaaS市場佔有率第一。金蝶是目前唯一入選Gartner全球市場指南(Market Guide)的中
國企業SaaS雲服務廠商，並成為唯一榮獲IDC 2020 ERP SaaS客戶滿意度大獎的中國SaaS廠商。

金蝶旗下的多款雲服務產品獲得標杆企業的青睞，包括金蝶雲•蒼穹（大企業數字共生平台）、金蝶雲•星空（中型企業智慧成長
服務平台）、金蝶雲•星辰
           （小微企業智慧成長服務平台）、雲之家
                            （智慧協同雲）、管易雲
                                      （企業電商雲服務平台） 、車商悅（汽車經
銷行業雲）
    及我家雲（物業行業雲）等。金蝶通過管理軟件與雲服務，已為世界範圍內超過680萬家企業、政府等組織提供服務。

traditional on-premise products and accelerate the transformation of subscription-based       向雲訂閱模式轉型，通過助力企業重構數字戰鬥力，
cloud services. By helping enterprise customers to empower digital capability, discover       釋放企業禁錮價值，成就客戶，決勝雲端，實現了雲
hidden value, as well as focusing on customer success and leading in the cloud market,        業務45.6%的高速增長。

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告


2020 is a year of responsibilities and rewards for Kingdee. In the backdrop of               2020是金蝶勇擔責任、滿載而歸的一年。面對疫情爆
pandemic outbreak, Kingdee donated RMB10 million through China Siyuan Foundation             發，金蝶除了通過中華思源工程扶貧基金會直接捐
for Poverty Alleviation. In addition, Kingdee supported Jointown Pharmaceutical who          贈人民幣1,000萬元，還助力奮戰一線的九州通醫藥公
fought the pandemic in the frontline, to achieve a full process management on medical        司，實現對醫療物資的全流程管理。面對中小微企業
materials and equipment. Kingdee has been consistently helping and supporting medium,        生存困境，金蝶始終風雨同行，除了入選超過50個省
small and micro enterprises, which were in tough time for survival. Kingdee was selected     市政府推薦產品名錄，加入工信部中小企業數字化賦
into the product recommendation list in over 50 provincial and municipal governments,        能專項行動，還發佈了中小微企業智慧成長計劃，推
and participated in the SME digital empowerment special action. Kingdee also launched        出中小微企業扶持優惠政策，全力幫助中小微企業實
the SME Smart Growth Project, implemented SME Support Preferential Policies and              現復工複產。面對困難和挑戰，金蝶始終堅持以客戶
committed to help small, medium and micro enterprises achieve the resumption of              為中心，以奮鬥者為本的哲學理念，與客戶並肩而

   Credit risk                                                                                信貸風險

   instruments carried at amortised cost, at fair value through profit or loss (FVPL)         計量的債務工具合同現金流量、以公允價值計
   and deposits with banks and financial institutions, as well as credit exposures to         量且其變動計入損益的債務工具合同現金流
   customers, including outstanding receivables.                                              量、銀行和其他金融機構存款以及未收應收款
                                                                                              在內的客戶信用風險。

   The management manages the credit risk of cash and cash equivalents, pledged               為管理來自現金及現金等價物、抵押存款、短
   and short-term bank deposits, long-term bank deposits and wealth management                期銀行存款、長期銀行存款及理財產品（歸類
   products (classified as financial assets at FVPL) by transacting with state-owned          為以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資
   financial institutions and reputable commercial banks which are all high-credit-           產）的信用風險，本集團管理層僅與中國國有或
   quality financial institutions in the PRC and Hong Kong.                                   信譽良好的中國大陸及香港的財務機構進行交
                                                                                              易。

   In relation to trade receivables and contract assets, the Group has two kinds of           對於應收賬款及合同資產，本集團有兩種銷售
   distribution channels, one is sales to distributors and the other is sales to end          渠道，一種是銷售給經銷商，另一種是銷售給
   customers.                                                                                 最終客戶。

   Individual risk limits are set based on internal or external ratings in accordance         因素。根據評估的信用品質設定相應的信用額

   regularly monitored by management.                                                         在信用額度內採購。

     Fund and working capital management                                                        資金及營運資金管理

      Our funds and liquidity management are centrally carried out by our fund                  資金及流動資金由資金管理部統一管理。資金
      management department. Our fund management department is generally                        管理部一般負責資金的整體管理及實施，包括
      responsible for overall management and implementation of funds, including                 制定本集團資金管理政策、指導、協調及規範
      formulating the capital management policy for our Group, guiding, coordinating            地區公司資金管理、制定年度資金計劃、檢討
      and standardizing the fund management of regional companies, making annual                及總結年度資本預算、監督及評估各地區公司
      funding plans, reviewing and summarizing annual capital budget, overseeing and            資金管理。地區公司的財務部致力實施總部制
      assessing fund management of each regional company. Our finance departments of            定的資金管理政策，負責於附屬公司層面制定
      regional companies are committed to implementing the fund management policies             資金計劃和執行資金營運。我們亦採取精細資
      formulated by our headquarters and are responsible for making funding plans and           金管理政策及實施一套資金管理規則和指引，
      executing capital operations at subsidiary level. We have also adopted sophisticated      例如《集團資金基礎內控管理辦法》與《分支機
      fund management policies and implemented a set of rules and guidelines on fund            構資金結算管理辦法》，以提高資金管理的效
      management, such as the Group Capital Internal Control Management Measures                果及效率，從而確保財政安全和減少資金成
     (《集團資金基礎內控管理辦法》) and Fund Settlement Of Branch Offices                                     本。
      Management Measures (《分支機構資金結算管理辦法》), to enhance the
      effectiveness and efficiency of fund management, thereby ensuring our financial
      security and reducing cost of capital.

     To manage our idle cash on hand, we purchase and redeem wealth management                  為了管理庫存閑置現金，我們購買及贖回理財
     products using them as our “cash pool” from which we could readily access cash             產品作「現金池」，我們可在需要時從中取得現
     as needed and generate higher yield than bank deposits. The underlying financial           金，獲得較銀行存款高的收益。我們投資的理
     assets of the wealth management products in which we invested primarily consist            財產品的相關金融資產主要包括金融機構發行

     amount of the purchase will be determined based on our surplus funds. Our main             釐定。我們以《資金理財管理辦法》作為對基金
     treasury policy on fund management is the Wealth Management Administration                 管理的主要財務政策。我們購買理財產品和管
     Solution (《資金理財管理辦法》). We consistently comply with our treasury                            理相關部門的程式與進行業務、會計及備案的
     policy during the procedures of purchasing the wealth management products and              過程一直遵守財務政策。
     managing the relevant departments, as well as in conducting business, accounting
     and filing.

     We are committed to safeguarding overall financial security and maintaining a              我們致力保障全面的財務安全，並且維持良好
     strong cash position and a healthy debt profile with strong repayment ability. By          的現金水準和穩健的負債結構，有充足的償付
     adopting a full, reasonable and professional assessment mechanism, preparing               能力。通過全面、合理及專業的評審機制，我
     annual and monthly funding plans, we have established disciplined fund                     們制定年度與每月資金規劃，已建立一套嚴謹
     management principal, which allows us to efficiently manage market risks.                  的資金管理原則，可以有效管理市場風險。

     For budget management, we have established a monthly, quarterly and annual                 預算管理方面，我們已建立每月、每季及全年

Share Award Scheme                                                                                   股份獎勵計劃

The share award scheme (the “Share Award Scheme”) was adopted by the Company on 4                   本公司二零一五年十二月四日採納之股份獎勵計劃
December 2015 to provide incentives and reward the contributions of certain employees              （
                                                                                                   「股份獎勵計劃」）以提供激勵以及獎勵本集團若干僱
and directors of the Group. The Share Award Scheme shall be valid and effective for a               員及董事所作的貢獻。股份獎勵計劃自採納之日起有
period of 10 years commencing from the adoption date. The Board may, from time to time,             效期為十年。董事會經考慮彼等認為合適的多項因素
in its absolute discretion select the Selected Participants after taking into various factors as    後可不時全權酌情挑選獲選參與者，並釐訂授予各獲
they deem appropriate and determine the number of Award Shares to be awarded to each of             選參與者的獎勵股份數目。獎勵股份將包括受託人使
the Selected Participants. the Awarded Shares will be comprised of Shares subscribed for            用以本公司自有資金為受託人準備的現金認購或購買
or purchased by the Trustee out of cash arranged by the Company out of the Company’s                的股份，受託人將以信託形式代獲選參與者持有獎勵
funds to the Trustee and be held on trust for the Selected Participants until such Shares are       股份，直至該等股份根據股份獎勵計劃的條文歸屬予
vested with the Selected Participants in accordance with the provisions of the Share Award          獲選參與者為止。
Scheme.

The Board shall not make any further award of Awarded Shares which will result in the                倘進一步授出獎勵股份會導致董事會根據計劃授出的
nominal value of the Shares awarded by the Board under the Scheme exceeding 3% of the                股份面值超過本公司不時已發行股本的3%，則董事
issued share capital of the Company from time to time. For the avoidance of doubt, the 3%            會不得進一步授出獎勵股份。為免生疑問，以上規
provided above shall exclude any Shares which have been vested in Selected Participants.             定的3%不應包括任何已歸屬予獲選參與者的股份。
The maximum number of shares which may be awarded to a Selected Participant under the                根據計劃於任何十二(12)個月期間可授予每名獲選參
Scheme in any twelve (12)-month period shall not exceed 1% of the issued share capital               與者的股份上限不得超過本公司不時已發行股本的
of the Company from time to time.                                                                    1%。

       loss.                                                                                           重大失誤和損失的保證。

      •      Risk response: The risk responsibility department properly uses risk                  •   風險應對：風險責任部門對已識別的重
             avoidance, reduction, sharing or tolerance to draw up a risk response                     大風險，結合集團風險承受度，妥善使
             scheme according to identified major risks and the Group’s risk tolerance,                用風險迴避、降低、分擔或承受等方法
             so that the Group can reasonably allocate resources for risk response and                 制定風險應對方案，以促使集團合理調
             reduce its overall risk level to an acceptable level;                                     配資源應對風險，使集團的整體風險水
                                                                                                       準降至可接受範圍；

      •      Risk control and reporting: The Group’s risks are controlled and reported             •   風險監控與報告：綜合利用風險預警指
             through comprehensive use of risk pre-warning indicators, internal audit                  標、內部審計、定期風險總結報告等形
             and regular risk summarization reports.                                                   式，對集團風險進行監控與報告。

      annual basis and to follow up the completion of any rectification discovered in              發現的整改完成情況。管理層審閱、評估了本
      internal control on a regular basis. The management has reviewed and assessed                集團風險管理及內部監控系統的有效性，該審

of international corporations and in light of the changing regulatory requirements               及加強實踐企業管治措施，以確保本公司長期持續發
and investors’ needs. This will also help in the long term to continuously develop the           展，提升企業價值。
Company, and enhance its corporate value.

Auditor’s Responsibilities for the Audit of the Consolidated                                   核數師就審計合併財務報表須承擔的責任
Financial Statements

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated financial     我們的目標，是對合併財務報表整體是否不存在由於
statements as a whole are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error,      欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保證，並
and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion solely       出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們僅向閣下（作
to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or        為整體）報告我們的意見，除此之外本報告別無其他
accept liability to any other person for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance     目的。我們不會就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上
is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance     或承擔任何責任。合理保證是高水準的保證，但不能

As part of an audit in accordance with ISAs, we exercise professional judgment and             在根據《國際審計準則》進行審計的過程中，我們運用

2.2   Prior year adjustments                                                                2.2   以前年度調整

      Prior to 1 January 2020, the Group recorded certain outsourcing service fees in             二零二零年一月一日之前，本集團在「銷售及
      relation to ERP and cloud implementation in “selling and marketing expenses”.               推廣費用」中記錄了與ERP及雲實施相關的某些
      During the year ended 31 December 2020, the Group reassessed the nature of                  外包服務費。截至二零二零年十二月三十一日
      the above outsourcing service and considered that these service fees charged by             止年度，本集團重新評估了上述外包服務的性
      outsourcers are directly related to ERP and cloud implementation services rendered          質，並認為外包商收取的這些服務費與本集團
      by the Group to its customers, thus they should be accounted for as and recorded            向客戶提供的ERP及雲實施服務直接相關，因
      in “cost of sales”. Adjustments have been made to reclassify outsourcing service            此應作為並記錄在「銷售成本」中。已作出調整
      fees of RMB270,370,000 which were previously recorded in “selling and marketing             將原於截至二零一九年十二月三十一日止年度
      expenses” for the year ended 31 December 2019 to “cost of sales” to conform with            的「銷售及推廣費用」入賬的外包服務費用人民
      the current year presentation. These changes have been applied retrospectively in           幣270,370,000元重分類至「銷售成本」，以符合本
      accordance with IAS 8 and there were no net impact on the profit for the year ended         年度的呈報方式。這些變更已按照國際會計準

2.3   Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                          2.3   合併與權益會計原則

2.3.1 Subsidiaries                                                                               2.3.1 子公司

      Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which the Group has           子公司為本集團所控制的實體（包括結構化實
      control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights               體）。當本集團從或有權從與該實體的參與中獲
      to, variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect          得變動收益並有能力對這些收益產生影響時，
      those returns through its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are         則其控制該子公司。自控制權轉移至本集團之
      fully consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the Group. They              日起，子公司被合併。其自喪失實際控制權之
      are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                                            日起停止合併。

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                             本集團的業務合併採用會計購買法核算（見附註
      combinations by the Group (refer to Note 2.4).                                                   2.4）
                                                                                                          。

      Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on transactions                        集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及交易的未變現
      between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated                    利得予以對銷。未變現損失亦予以對銷，除非
      unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.              交易提供所轉撥資產的減值證據。子公司報告
      Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                  的數額已按需要作出改變，以確保與本集團採
      consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                                              用的政策符合一致。

      Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries are shown                    子公司業績和股權中的非控股權益分別列示於
      separately in the consolidated income statement, consolidated statement of                       合併利潤表、合併綜合收益表、合併股權變動
      comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and consolidated statement                  表和合併財務狀況合併表。
      of financial position respectively.

2.3.2 Associates                                                                                 2.3.2 聯營

      Associates are all entities over which the Group has significant influence but not               聯營公司是集團對其有重大影響但不控制或聯
      control or joint control. This is generally the case where the Group holds between               合控制的所有實體。通常情況下，該集團擁有
      20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are accounted for                    20%至50%的投票權。聯營公司的投資在最初
      using the equity method of accounting (see Note 2.3.3 below), after initially being              按成本確認後，以權益會計方法核算（見附註
      recognised at cost.                                                                              2.3.3）
                                                                                                            。

2.3.3 Equity method                                                                              2.3.3 權益法

      Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially recognised at               權益法下，投資帳面價值最初以成本確認，之
      cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the post-acquisition              後卻按本集團持有投資對象得份額確認投資利
      profits or losses of the investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of                    潤或虧損以及其他綜合收益。從聯營公司收到
      movements in other comprehensive income of the investee in other comprehensive                   或應收的股息確認為投資帳面價值的減少。
      income. Dividends received or receivable from associates are recognised as a
      reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.

      Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment equals                       若本集團在股本投資中所承擔的損失與本集團

      losses/gains — net’.                                                                           損╱收益－淨額」中的損益中確認。

      the counterparty.                                                                              破產時，這也必須具有約束力。

2.23 Current and deferred income tax                                                              2.23 當期及遞延所得稅

      The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on the current                 當期的所得稅費用或抵免額，是根據各司法管
      period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for each                          轄區的適用所得稅率，根據當期應納稅所得額
      jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable to           應納稅所得額，該稅率由因暫時性差異和未使
      temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                                                   用的稅損引起的遞延所得稅資產和負債的變化
                                                                                                        而調整。

(a)   Current income tax                                                                          (a)   當期所得稅

      The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the tax laws enacted                  當期所得稅費用是根據在公司及其子公司經營
      or substantively enacted at the statement of financial position date in the countries             並產生應納稅所得額的國家╱地區在財務狀況
      where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and generate taxable income.                       表日期已頒佈或實質上已頒佈的稅法計算的。
      Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect                     管理層會就適用稅法受到解釋的情況定期評估
      to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation and                 納稅申報表中的頭寸，並考慮稅務機關是否有
      considers whether it is probable that a taxation authority will accept an uncertain               可能接受不確定的稅收待遇。本集團根據最可
      tax treatment. The Group measures its tax balances either based on the most likely                能的金額或預期價值計量其稅收餘額，具體取
      amount or the expected value, depending on which method provides a better                         決於哪種方法可以更好地預測不確定性的解決
      prediction of the resolution of the uncertainty.                                                  方法。

(b)   Deferred income tax                                                                         (b)   遞延所得稅

      Deferred income tax is recognised, using the liability method, on temporary                       遞延所得稅採用負債法，對資產和負債的計稅
      differences arising between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying            基礎與其在合併財務報表中的賬面價值之間產
      amounts in the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred tax liabilities               生的暫時性差異予以確認。但是，如果遞延稅
      are not recognised if they arise from the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred           項負債是由於初始確認商譽而產生的，則不予
      income tax is not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset or              確認；如果遞延所得稅是由於在企業合併以外
      liability in a transaction other than a business combination that at the time of the              的交易中對資產或負債的初始確認而產生的，
      transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss. Deferred income                則不計入遞延所得稅。該交易既不影響會計也
      tax is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or substantially              不影響應稅損益。遞延所得稅是使用在財務狀
      enacted by the statement of financial position date and are expected to apply when                況表日期已經制定或大致制定的稅率（和法律）
      the related deferred income tax asset is realised or the deferred income tax liability is         確定的，預計在實現相關遞延所得稅資產或清
      settled.                                                                                          算遞延所得稅負債時適用。

2.26 Provisions                                                                                      2.26 準備

      Provisions are recognised when: the Group has a present legal or constructive                        當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法律或
      obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an outflow of resources will              推定義務；很可能需要有資源的流出以結算義
      be required to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.                    務；及金額已被可靠估計時，當作出準備。準
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.                                           備不會就未來經營虧損確認準備。

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an outflow will                 如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中有資源流
      be required in settlement is determined by considering the class of obligations as a                 出的可能性，則可根據義務的類別整體考慮。
      whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to                即使在同一義務類別所包含的任何一個項目相
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be small.                                 關的資源流出的可能性極低，仍須確認準備。

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s best estimate of                        撥備按管理層於報告期末償還現時義務所需開
      the expenditure required to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting                支的最佳估計的現值計量。用於確定現值的折
      period. The discount rate used to determine the present value is a pre-tax rate                      現率是稅前利率，其反映了貨幣時間價值和負
      that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                    債特定風險的當前市場評估。隨著時間的流
      specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to the passage of time is               逝，準備金的增加被確認為利息支出。
      recognised as interest expense.

2.29 Interest income                                                                          2.29 利息收益

      Interest income from financial assets at FVPL calculated using the effective interest         依有效利息方式計算的以公允價值計量且其變
      method is recognised in the income statement as realised gains on financial assets            動計入損益的金融資產利息收入已在損益表中
      at FVPL within “Other losses/gains – net”.                                                    確認為包含在」其他虧損╱利得－淨額」的淨值
                                                                                                    中以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益的金融資
                                                                                                    產的實現利得。

      Interest income on financial assets at amortised cost calculated using the effective          使用實際利率法計算的以攤餘成本計量的金融
      interest method is recognised in profit or loss as part of other income.                      資產的利息收入在損益中確認為其他收入的一

     the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease components based on             集團根據相對獨立價格將合同中的對價分配給

     other than the security interests in the leased assets that are held by the lessor.          租賃資產中的擔保權益外，租賃協議不施加任
     Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                            何其他約定。租賃資產不得用作借貸擔保。

            incentives receivable;                                                                      任何應收的租賃激勵；

     •      variable lease payment that are based on an index or a rate, initially                •     基於指數或比率確定的可變租賃付款
            measured using the index or rate as at the commencement date;                               額，採用租賃期開始日的指數或比率進

            guarantees;

3.3   Capital risk management                                                                3.3   資金風險管理

      The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability            本集團的資金管理政策，是保障集團能持續運
      to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns for owners and benefits           營，以為股東提供回報和為其他權利持有人提
      for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the            供利益，同時維持最佳的資本結構以降低資金
      cost of capital.                                                                             成本。

      In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the               為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調整
      amount of dividends paid to owners, return capital to shareholders, issue new                支付予股東的股利數額、向股東退還資本、發
      shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                                        行新股或出售資產以減低債務。

      Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital on the basis              與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用資本負債比
      of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital.         率監察其資本。此比率按照債務淨額除以總資
      Net debt is calculated as total borrowings (including “current and non-current               本計算。債務淨額為總借款（包括合併財務狀況
      borrowings” as shown in the consolidated statement of financial position) less               表所列的「流動及非流動借款」
                                                                                                                ）減去現金和現金
      cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is calculated as “equity” as shown in the           等價物。總資本為「權益」
                                                                                                              （如合併財務狀況表所
      consolidated statement of financial position plus net debt.                                  列）
                                                                                                    加債務淨額。

      The gearing ratios at 31 December 2020 and 2019 were as follows:                             在二零二零年及二零一九年十二月三十一日，

      The loss allowances for financial assets are based on assumptions about risk                  金融資產的損失準備金是基於對違約風險和預
      of default and expected loss rates. The Group uses judgement in making these                  期損失率的假設。本集團根據過去的歷史、現
      assumptions and selecting the inputs to the impairment calculation, based on the              有的市場狀況，以及在每個報告期結束時的前
      Group’s past history, existing market conditions as well as forward looking estimates         瞻性估計，在作出這些假設和選擇減值計算的
      at the end of each reporting period. Details of the key assumptions and inputs used           輸入時使用判斷。所使用的主要假設和投入的
      are disclosed in the tables in Note 3.1(b).                                                   詳情載於附註3.1(b)的表格。

    Impairment test for goodwill                                                                 商譽減值

    Goodwill arises from the Group’s acquisition of Shanghai Guanyi Cloudcomputing               商譽來自於本集團對上海管易雲計算軟件有限
    Software Co., Ltd. (“Guanyi”) and was determined at the date of acquisition on 29            公司的收購，其在二零一六年二月二十九日收
    February 2016, being the difference between the purchase considerations and the              購日確認。該數值為管易雲可辨認淨資產公允
    fair value of net identifiable assets of Guanyi. Goodwill has been allocated to the          價值與支付對價的差額。對管易合併產生的商
    CGU of Guanyi for impairment testing.                                                        譽已經通過現金產出單元進行了減值測試。

    The recoverable amount of a CGU is determined based on the higher of its fair                現金產生單位的可收回金額是基於其公允價
    value less cost of disposal and value-in-use. The higher amount represents                   值減去處置成本和使用價值兩者的較高者確
    the value-in-use, which is calculated using pre-tax cash flow forecasts based                定的。較高的金額為使用價值，使用價值是根
    on the financial budgets approved by management covering a five-year period.                 據管理層批准的涵蓋五年期間的財務預算，使
    Management determined a forecast period of five years based on expected                      用稅前現金流量預測得出的。管理層根據關義
    development trend of Guanyi and industry experience. Cash flows beyond the five-             的預期發展趨勢及行業經驗確定了五年的預測
    year period are extrapolated using the estimated terminal growth rate stated below.          期。超過五年期間的現金流量使用以下估算的
    The terminal growth rate does not exceed the long-term average growth rate for               最終增長率推斷。永續增長率不超過CGU運營
    the enterprise management cloud services business in which the CGU operates.                 的企業管理雲服務業務的長期平均增長率。所
    The discount rate used is pre-tax and reflects specific risks relating to the relevant       使用的折現率是稅前收益，反映了與相關行業
    industry.                                                                                    有關的特定風險。

    The key assumptions used for value-in-use calculations for 2020 and 2019 are as              二零二零和二零一九年使用價值計算使用的主
